Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The phylogeny operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 121. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 442 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_121(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 121. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^442(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any phylogeny module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 424. The projective resolution has length at most 14 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.
